EDeuteronomy 4:1-40 derivation 2026-09-16 — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 2, chapter 4The book's second reading, at the chapter's grain (chapter 4 as two drafts — this unit the exhortation, Horeb retold, no image, the exile and the return, the one God (4:1-40)): Onkelos Deuteronomy 4:1-40 whole and fresh; THE SIFREI ON DEUTERONOMY has NO PISKA on the chapter (30 on 3:29, 31 on 6:4) — its eight rows citing chapter 4 found by the scan of the whole export in both files, six read fresh (48:2, 49:2, 148:8, 301:21, 306:1, 323:1), two credited from sitting 1 (30:2, 37:9). Ledger deu_04_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md (one ledger for the two drafts), coverage computed by script, the ink facts computed from the Tanakh DB and the snapshot store (every fact an assert — six fell on the first typed pass, retyped from the leg print, none on the second), the engine's numeral parser measured on every verse (four number verses read, no gap), every quotation cut by consonants (no cut miss; the lint 0). Seated as claims DV04A-01..05: the exhortation: and now, israel, hear the statutes and the judgments i teach you, that you may live and possess the land; add nothing to the word, take nothing from it; your eyes saw baal-peor — every man who went after it destroyed, and you who cleave to the lord all alive this day; see, i taught you as the lord my god commanded me; keep and do, for that is your wisdom in the peoples' eyes — what great nation has god so near, what nation statutes so righteous as all this torah; horeb retold: only take heed and keep your soul, lest you forget what your eyes saw — make it known to your sons and sons' sons — the day you stood at horeb when the lord said, assemble the people that they may learn to fear me and teach their sons; you stood under the mountain burning to the heart of heaven; the lord spoke from the fire — a voice of words, no form; he declared his covenant, the ten words, and wrote them on two tablets of stone; and me he commanded to teach you statutes; no image: take heed to your souls, for you saw no form at horeb — lest you make a graven image, the form of any figure, the likeness of male or female, beast, bird, creeping thing, fish; lest you lift your eyes to the sun, moon and stars, all the host of heaven which the lord apportioned to all the peoples, and serve them; you the lord took out of the iron furnace to be his people; the lord was angry with me on your account and swore i should not cross — i die in this land; take heed lest you forget the covenant; for the lord your god is a consuming fire, a jealous god; the exile and the return: when you beget sons and grow old and corrupt yourselves with an image, i call heaven and earth to witness that you shall surely perish quickly, not prolong days, be utterly destroyed; the lord will scatter you, few in number among the nations, and there you shall serve gods of wood and stone that see not; and from there seek the lord and find him with all your heart and soul — in your distress, in the end of days, return and hearken; for he is a merciful god who will not fail you nor forget the covenant of your fathers; the one god: ask of the former days since god created man, from one end of heaven to the other — has a people heard the voice of god from the fire and lived, has a god taken a nation from a nation by trials, signs, wonders, war, a mighty hand, an outstretched arm and great terrors? you were shown to know the lord is god, none beside him; from heaven his voice, on earth his great fire; because he loved your fathers he chose their seed and brought you out with his presence; know this day and lay it to heart — the lord is god in heaven above and on earth beneath, none else; keep his statutes that it go well and you prolong days. The compile (the chapter's one law and one case; the three cities closing the refuge runner's debit; the receipt at 4:5 paying the register's seat; the retellings of Horeb, Baal-peor, the bar and the two kings through the readback's first form) follows in its own sitting (World/step9/DEUTERONOMY_WALK.md).tested

THE ONE GOD: ASK OF THE FORMER DAYS SINCE GOD CREATED MAN, FROM ONE END OF HEAVEN TO THE OTHER — HAS A PEOPLE HEARD THE VOICE OF GOD FROM THE FIRE AND LIVED, HAS A GOD TAKEN A NATION FROM A NATION BY TRIALS, SIGNS, WONDERS, WAR, A MIGHTY HAND, AN OUTSTRETCHED ARM AND GREAT TERRORS? YOU WERE SHOWN TO KNOW THE LORD IS GOD, NONE BESIDE HIM; FROM HEAVEN HIS VOICE, ON EARTH HIS GREAT FIRE; BECAUSE HE LOVED YOUR FATHERS HE CHOSE THEIR SEED AND BROUGHT YOU OUT WITH HIS PRESENCE; KNOW THIS DAY AND LAY IT TO HEART — THE LORD IS GOD IN HEAVEN ABOVE AND ON EARTH BENEATH, NONE ELSE; KEEP HIS STATUTES THAT IT GO WELL AND YOU PROLONG DAYS [claim DV04A-05].
